#21 Read/workspace/submission/model.py
38	    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
39	    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)
40	
41	
42	def _norm(x):
43	    return F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],))
44	
45	
46	class Block(nn.Module):
47	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
48	        super().__init__()
49	        d = cfg.d_model
50	        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
51	        self.head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
52	        self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(3, d, d))
53	        self.proj = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, d))
54	        self.mlp_type = cfg.mlp_type
55	        n_in = 2 if cfg.mlp_type == "swiglu" else 1
56	        self.fc = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(n_in, cfg.mlp_width, d))
57	        self.fc_out = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, cfg.mlp_width))
58	        self.qk_norm = cfg.qk_norm
59	        # per-loop parameters: norm gains, residual scales and input injection
60	        n = cfg.n_loops
61	        self.g_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
62	        self.g_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
63	        self.s_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
64	        self.s_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
65	        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.inject else None
66	        std = 0.5 / math.sqrt(d)
67	        nn.init.uniform_(self.qkv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
68	        nn.init.uniform_(self.fc, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
69	        nn.init.zeros_(self.proj)
70	        nn.init.zeros_(self.fc_out)
71	
72	    def forward(self, x, x0, cos, sin, i: int):
73	        batch, length, width = x.shape
74	        if self.inj is not None:
75	            x = x + self.inj[i] * x0
76	        h = _norm(x) * self.g_attn[i]
77	        q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
78	        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
79	        if self.qk_norm:
80	            q, k = _norm(q), _norm(k)
81	        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
82	        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
83	        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width)
84	        x = x + self.s_attn[i] * F.linear(out, self.proj)
85	        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]
86	        if self.mlp_type == "swiglu":
87	            a, b = F.linear(h, self.fc.flatten(0, 1)).chunk(2, dim=-1)
88	            u = F.silu(a) * b
89	        elif self.mlp_type == "relu2":
90	            u = F.relu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0])).square()
91	        else:
92	            u = F.gelu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0]), approximate="tanh")
93	        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out)
94	
95	
96	class LoopedLM(nn.Module):
97	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
98	        super().__init__()
99	        self.cfg = cfg
100	        d = cfg.d_model
101	        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)
102	        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)
103	        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_unique))
104	        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
105	        self.out_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
106	        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
107	        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
108	        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
109	        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py may replace this with a compiled version
110	
111	    def apply_blocks(self, x, cos, sin):
112	        x0 = x
113	        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
114	            for block in self.blocks:
115	                x = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i)
116	        return _norm(x) * self.out_gain
117	
118	    def forward(self, input_ids):
119	        length = input_ids.shape[1]
120	        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
121	        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
122	        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
123	        x = _norm(self.embed(input_ids)) * self.in_scale
124	        return self.stack(x, angles.cos(), angles.sin())  # the harness applies the tied output head
125	
126	
127	def build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:
128	    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
129	
130	    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
131

p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    embed_std: float = 0.02
""", """    embed_std: float = 0.02
    value_residual: bool = False
    mem_at: tuple = ()  # loop indices after which an embedding-memory layer is applied
    mem_temp: float = 1.0
""")
s = s.replace("""        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.inject else None
        std""", """        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.inject else None
        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.value_residual else None
        std""")
s = s.replace("""    def forward(self, x, x0, cos, sin, i: int):
        batch, length, width = x.shape""", """    def forward(self, x, x0, cos, sin, i: int, v0=None):
        batch, length, width = x.shape""")
s = s.replace("""        q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view""", """        q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
        if self.v_mix is not None:
            if v0 is None:
                v0 = v
            else:
                v = v + self.v_mix[i] * (v0 - v)
        q, k, v = (t.view""")
s = s.replace("""        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out)
""", """        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out), v0
""")
s = s.replace("""        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))""", """        self.mem_at = tuple(cfg.mem_at)
        if self.mem_at:
            self.mem_g = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(len(self.mem_at), d))
            self.mem_s = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(len(self.mem_at), d))
        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))""")
s = s.replace("""        x0 = x
        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
            for block in self.blocks:
                x = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i)
""", """        x0 = x
        v0 = None
        table = self.embed.weight
        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
            for block in self.blocks:
                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)
            if i in self.mem_at:
                j = self.mem_at.index(i)
                scores = F.linear(_norm(x) * self.mem_g[j], table) * self.cfg.mem_temp
                x = x + self.mem_s[j] * F.linear(torch.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).type_as(scores), table.t())
""")
open(p, "w").write(s)

#26 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    mem_temp: float = 1.0
""", """    mem_temp: float = 1.0
    mem_act: str = "softmax"
""")
s = s.replace("""                scores = F.linear(_norm(x) * self.mem_g[j], table) * self.cfg.mem_temp
                x = x + self.mem_s[j] * F.linear(torch.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).type_as(scores), table.t())
""", """                scores = F.linear(_norm(x) * self.mem_g[j], table) * self.cfg.mem_temp
                if self.cfg.mem_act == "softmax":
                    weights = torch.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).type_as(scores)
                else:
                    weights = F.relu(scores).square()
                x = x + self.mem_s[j] * F.linear(weights, table.t())
""")
open(p, "w").write(s)

#33 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    smear: bool = False""", """    hid_gain: bool = False  # per-loop gain on the MLP hidden units
    q_gain: bool = False  # per-loop learnable gain on the normalised queries (attention temperature)
    smear: bool = False""")
s = s.replace("""        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.value_residual else None
""", """        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.value_residual else None
        self.hid_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, cfg.mlp_width)) if cfg.hid_gain else None
        self.q_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, self.n_heads, 1, 1)) if cfg.q_gain else None
""")
s = s.replace("""        if self.qk_norm:
            q, k = _norm(q), _norm(k)
""", """        if self.qk_norm:
            q, k = _norm(q), _norm(k)
        if self.q_gain is not None:
            q = q * self.q_gain[i]
""")
s = s.replace("""        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out), v0""", """        if self.hid_gain is not None:
            u = u * self.hid_gain[i]
        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out), v0""")
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF

Tied MLP was clearly worse (3.754) — dropped. Since per-loop specialization pays off, I'll test more cheap per-loop parameters: per-loop head-output gains and per-loop MLP hidden biases.

Ran 12 commandsBash ×12 (7m)
#46 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    mlp_tied: bool = False""", """    v_gain: bool = False  # per-loop gain on the attention output before the projection (head gating)
    hid_bias: bool = False  # per-loop bias on the MLP hidden units
    mlp_tied: bool = False""")
s = s.replace("""        self.q_gain = nn.Parameter""", """        self.v_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d)) if cfg.v_gain else None
        self.hid_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, cfg.mlp_width)) if cfg.hid_bias else None
        self.q_gain = nn.Parameter""")
s = s.replace("""        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width)
""", """        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width)
        if self.v_gain is not None:
            out = out * self.v_gain[i]
""")
s = s.replace("""        elif self.mlp_type == "relu2":
            u = F.relu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0])).square()""", """        elif self.mlp_type == "relu2":
            pre = F.linear(h, self.fc[0])
            if self.hid_bias is not None:
                pre = pre + self.hid_bias[i]
            u = F.relu(pre).square()""")
open(p, "w").write(s)
import json
p = "submission/config.json"
c = json.load(open(p))
c["model"].update(hid_gain=True, q_gain=True, mlp_width=1836)
json.dump(c, open(p, "w"), indent=1)
EOF

#51 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    n_heads: int = 8
""", """    n_heads: int = 8
    n_kv_heads: int = 0  # 0: same as n_heads; fewer heads share keys and values (GQA)
""")
s = s.replace("""        self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(3, d, d))
""", """        self.n_kv = cfg.n_kv_heads or cfg.n_heads
        self.kv_dim = self.n_kv * self.head_dim
        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
            self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(3, d, d))
        else:
            self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, d))  # queries only
            self.kv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(2, self.kv_dim, d))
""")
s = s.replace("""        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.value_residual else None""",
"""        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, self.kv_dim)) if cfg.value_residual else None""")
s = s.replace("""        nn.init.uniform_(self.qkv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
""", """        nn.init.uniform_(self.qkv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
        if self.n_kv != self.n_heads:
            nn.init.uniform_(self.kv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
""")
s = s.replace("""        q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
""", """        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
            q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
        else:
            q = F.linear(h, self.qkv)
            k, v = F.linear(h, self.kv.flatten(0, 1)).split(self.kv_dim, dim=2)
""")
s = s.replace("""        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
""", """        q = q.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_kv, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (k, v))
""")
s = s.replace("""        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
""", """        if self.n_kv != self.n_heads:
            k = k.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)
            v = v.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
""")
open(p, "w").write(s)

While those run, I'm implementing optional per-loop low-rank adapters (LoRA) on the attention and MLP input projections — a step beyond the per-loop diagonal gains that have been paying off.

Ran 2 commandsBash ×2 (16s)
#69 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    mlp_tied: bool = False""", """    lora_qkv: int = 0  # rank of per-loop low-rank corrections to the attention input projection
    lora_fc: int = 0  # rank of per-loop low-rank corrections to the MLP input projection
    mlp_tied: bool = False""")
s = s.replace("""        self.v_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d)) if cfg.v_gain else None
""", """        self.v_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d)) if cfg.v_gain else None
        r1, r2 = cfg.lora_qkv, cfg.lora_fc
        self.lora_qkv_down = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(n, r1, d) / math.sqrt(d)) if r1 else None
        self.lora_qkv_up = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, 3 * d, r1)) if r1 else None
        self.lora_fc_down = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(n, r2, d) / math.sqrt(d)) if r2 else None
        self.lora_fc_up = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, cfg.mlp_width, r2)) if r2 else None
""")
s = s.replace("""        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
            q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
""", """        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
            qkv = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1))
            if self.lora_qkv_up is not None:
                qkv = qkv + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_qkv_down[i]), self.lora_qkv_up[i])
            q, k, v = qkv.split(width, dim=2)
""")
s = s.replace("""            pre = F.linear(h, self.fc[0])
""", """            pre = F.linear(h, self.fc[0])
            if self.lora_fc_up is not None:
                pre = pre + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_fc_down[i]), self.lora_fc_up[i])
""")
open(p, "w").write(s)

p = "submission/train.py"
s = open(p).read()
old = """    others = [p for p in body.parameters() if id(p) not in ids]"""
new = """    lora = [p for n, p in body.named_parameters() if "lora" in n]
    ids |= {id(p) for p in lora}
    others = [p for p in body.parameters() if id(p) not in ids]"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
old = """        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
    ]"""
new = """        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
        {"params": lora, "lr": cfg.get("lora_lr", 0.003), "weight_decay": 0.0},
    ]"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(p, "w").write(s)

#75 Bashcd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace; python - <<'EOF'
p = "submission/model.py"
s = open(p).read()
s = s.replace("""    mlp_tied: bool = False""", """    dense: bool = False  # DenseFormer-style learned averaging over all earlier loop outputs
    mlp_tied: bool = False""")
s = s.replace("""        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))""", """        if cfg.dense:
            n = cfg.n_loops
            init = torch.zeros(n, n + 1)
            init[torch.arange(n), torch.arange(1, n + 1)] = 1.0
            self.dwa = nn.Parameter(init)
        else:
            self.dwa = None
        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))""")
old = """        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
            for block in self.blocks:
                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)
"""
new = """        history = [x0]
        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
            for block in self.blocks:
                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)
            if self.dwa is not None:
                history.append(x)
                x = sum(self.dwa[i, j] * h for j, h in enumerate(history))
"""
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(p, "w").write(s)
EOF

8 tool callsBash ×7, Read ×1 (7m)
#94 Read/workspace/submission/model.py
1	"""Looped (depth-recurrent) transformer with a wide, free token embedding.
2	
3	A small set of unique transformer blocks is applied several times in a row
4	(n_loops), so the model spends much more compute per token than it has
5	weights. Each application of a block gets its own tiny set of per-loop
6	parameters (norm gains and residual scales) so the loops can specialise.
7	RMSNorm, RoPE, QK-norm, ReLU^2 MLP, no biases. The model returns the final
8	hidden states; the harness turns them into logits with the token embedding
9	table (tied output head).
10	"""
11	
12	from __future__ import annotations
13	
14	import math
15	from dataclasses import dataclass
16	
17	import torch
18	import torch.nn.functional as F
19	from torch import nn
20	
21	
22	@dataclass
23	class ModelConfig:
24	    vocab_size: int = 8192
25	    d_model: int = 512
26	    n_heads: int = 8
27	    n_kv_heads: int = 0  # 0: same as n_heads; fewer heads share keys and values (GQA)
28	    n_unique: int = 1
29	    n_loops: int = 15
30	    mlp_width: int = 1856
31	    mlp_type: str = "relu2"
32	    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
33	    qk_norm: bool = True
34	    inject: bool = True
35	    embed_std: float = 0.02
36	    value_residual: bool = False
37	    mem_at: tuple = ()  # loop indices after which an embedding-memory layer is applied
38	    mem_temp: float = 1.0
39	    mem_act: str = "softmax"
40	    hid_gain: bool = False  # per-loop gain on the MLP hidden units
41	    q_gain_dim: bool = False  # make the per-loop query gain per dimension instead of per head
42	    q_gain: bool = False  # per-loop learnable gain on the normalised queries (attention temperature)
43	    v_gain: bool = False  # per-loop gain on the attention output before the projection (head gating)
44	    hid_bias: bool = False  # per-loop bias on the MLP hidden units
45	    lora_qkv: int = 0  # rank of per-loop low-rank corrections to the attention input projection
46	    lora_fc: int = 0  # rank of per-loop low-rank corrections to the MLP input projection
47	    dense: bool = False  # DenseFormer-style learned averaging over all earlier loop outputs
48	    mlp_tied: bool = False  # MLP down-projection is the up-projection transposed
49	    smear: bool = False  # add a gated copy of the previous token's embedding to the input
50	
51	
52	def _rotate_half(x):
53	    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
54	    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)
55	
56	
57	def _norm(x):
58	    return F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],))
59	
60	
61	class Block(nn.Module):
62	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
63	        super().__init__()
64	        d = cfg.d_model
65	        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
66	        self.head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
67	        self.n_kv = cfg.n_kv_heads or cfg.n_heads
68	        self.kv_dim = self.n_kv * self.head_dim
69	        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
70	            self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(3, d, d))
71	        else:
72	            self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, d))  # queries only
73	            self.kv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(2, self.kv_dim, d))
74	        self.proj = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, d))
75	        self.mlp_type = cfg.mlp_type
76	        n_in = 2 if cfg.mlp_type == "swiglu" else 1
77	        self.fc = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(n_in, cfg.mlp_width, d))
78	        self.fc_out = None if cfg.mlp_tied else nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, cfg.mlp_width))
79	        self.qk_norm = cfg.qk_norm
80	        # per-loop parameters: norm gains, residual scales and input injection
81	        n = cfg.n_loops
82	        self.g_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
83	        self.g_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
84	        self.s_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
85	        self.s_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d) if cfg.mlp_tied else torch.ones(n, d))
86	        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.inject else None
87	        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, self.kv_dim)) if cfg.value_residual else None
88	        self.hid_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, cfg.mlp_width)) if cfg.hid_gain else None
89	        self.v_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d)) if cfg.v_gain else None
90	        r1, r2 = cfg.lora_qkv, cfg.lora_fc
91	        self.lora_qkv_down = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(n, r1, d) / math.sqrt(d)) if r1 else None
92	        self.lora_qkv_up = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, 3 * d, r1)) if r1 else None
93	        self.lora_fc_down = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(n, r2, d) / math.sqrt(d)) if r2 else None
94	        self.lora_fc_up = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, cfg.mlp_width, r2)) if r2 else None
95	        self.hid_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, cfg.mlp_width)) if cfg.hid_bias else None
96	        q_shape = (n, self.n_heads, 1, self.head_dim if cfg.q_gain_dim else 1)
97	        self.q_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(q_shape)) if cfg.q_gain else None
98	        std = 0.5 / math.sqrt(d)
99	        nn.init.uniform_(self.qkv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
100	        if self.n_kv != self.n_heads:
101	            nn.init.uniform_(self.kv, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
102	        nn.init.uniform_(self.fc, -std * math.sqrt(3), std * math.sqrt(3))
103	        nn.init.zeros_(self.proj)
104	        if self.fc_out is not None:
105	            nn.init.zeros_(self.fc_out)
106	
107	    def forward(self, x, x0, cos, sin, i: int, v0=None):
108	        batch, length, width = x.shape
109	        if self.inj is not None:
110	            x = x + self.inj[i] * x0
111	        h = _norm(x) * self.g_attn[i]
112	        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:
113	            qkv = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1))
114	            if self.lora_qkv_up is not None:
115	                qkv = qkv + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_qkv_down[i]), self.lora_qkv_up[i])
116	            q, k, v = qkv.split(width, dim=2)
117	        else:
118	            q = F.linear(h, self.qkv)
119	            k, v = F.linear(h, self.kv.flatten(0, 1)).split(self.kv_dim, dim=2)
120	        if self.v_mix is not None:
121	            if v0 is None:
122	                v0 = v
123	            else:
124	                v = v + self.v_mix[i] * (v0 - v)
125	        q = q.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
126	        k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_kv, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (k, v))
127	        if self.qk_norm:
128	            q, k = _norm(q), _norm(k)
129	        if self.q_gain is not None:
130	            q = q * self.q_gain[i]
131	        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
132	        if self.n_kv != self.n_heads:
133	            k = k.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)
134	            v = v.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)
135	        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
136	        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width)
137	        if self.v_gain is not None:
138	            out = out * self.v_gain[i]
139	        x = x + self.s_attn[i] * F.linear(out, self.proj)
140	        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]
141	        if self.mlp_type == "swiglu":
142	            a, b = F.linear(h, self.fc.flatten(0, 1)).chunk(2, dim=-1)
143	            u = F.silu(a) * b
144	        elif self.mlp_type == "relu2":
145	            pre = F.linear(h, self.fc[0])
146	            if self.lora_fc_up is not None:
147	                pre = pre + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_fc_down[i]), self.lora_fc_up[i])
148	            if self.hid_bias is not None:
149	                pre = pre + self.hid_bias[i]
150	            u = F.relu(pre).square()
151	        else:
152	            u = F.gelu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0]), approximate="tanh")
153	        if self.hid_gain is not None:
154	            u = u * self.hid_gain[i]
155	        w_out = self.fc[0].t() if self.fc_out is None else self.fc_out
156	        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, w_out), v0
157	
158	
159	class LoopedLM(nn.Module):
160	    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
161	        super().__init__()
162	        self.cfg = cfg
163	        d = cfg.d_model
164	        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)
165	        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)
166	        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_unique))
167	        self.mem_at = tuple(cfg.mem_at)
168	        if self.mem_at:
169	            self.mem_g = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(len(self.mem_at), d))
170	            self.mem_s = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(len(self.mem_at), d))
171	        self.smear = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d)) if cfg.smear else None
172	        if cfg.dense:
173	            n = cfg.n_loops
174	            init = torch.zeros(n, n + 1)
175	            init[torch.arange(n), torch.arange(1, n + 1)] = 1.0
176	            self.dwa = nn.Parameter(init)
177	        else:
178	            self.dwa = None
179	        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
180	        self.out_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
181	        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
182	        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
183	        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
184	        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py may replace this with a compiled version
185	
186	    def apply_blocks(self, x, cos, sin):
187	        x0 = x
188	        v0 = None
189	        table = self.embed.weight
190	        history = [x0]
191	        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
192	            for block in self.blocks:
193	                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)
194	            if self.dwa is not None:
195	                history.append(x)
196	                x = sum(self.dwa[i, j] * h for j, h in enumerate(history))
197	            if i in self.mem_at:
198	                j = self.mem_at.index(i)
199	                scores = F.linear(_norm(x) * self.mem_g[j], table) * self.cfg.mem_temp
200	                if self.cfg.mem_act == "softmax":
201	                    weights = torch.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).type_as(scores)
202	                else:
203	                    weights = F.relu(scores).square()
204	                x = x + self.mem_s[j] * F.linear(weights, table.t())
205	        return _norm(x) * self.out_gain
206	
207	    def forward(self, input_ids):
208	        length = input_ids.shape[1]
209	        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
210	        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
211	        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
212	        x = _norm(self.embed(input_ids)) * self.in_scale
213	        if self.smear is not None:
214	            x = x + self.smear * F.pad(x[:, :-1], (0, 0, 1, 0))
215	        return self.stack(x, angles.cos(), angles.sin())  # the harness applies the tied output head
216	
217	
218	def build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:
219	    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
220	
221	    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
222

v0=None):\n        batch, length, width = x.shape\n        if self.inj is not None:\n            x = x + self.inj[i] * x0\n        h = _norm(x) * self.g_attn[i]\n        if self.n_kv == self.n_heads:\n            qkv = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1))\n            if self.lora_qkv_up is not None:\n                qkv = qkv + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_qkv_down[i]), self.lora_qkv_up[i])\n            q, k, v = qkv.split(width, dim=2)\n        else:\n            q = F.linear(h, self.qkv)\n            k, v = F.linear(h, self.kv.flatten(0, 1)).split(self.kv_dim, dim=2)\n        if self.v_mix is not None:\n            if v0 is None:\n                v0 = v\n            else:\n                v = v + self.v_mix[i] * (v0 - v)\n        q = q.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)\n        k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_kv, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (k, v))\n        if self.qk_norm:\n            q, k = _norm(q), _norm(k)\n        if self.q_gain is not None:\n            q = q * self.q_gain[i]\n        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin\n        if self.n_kv != self.n_heads:\n            k = k.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)\n            v = v.repeat_interleave(self.n_heads // self.n_kv, dim=1)\n        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)\n        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width)\n        if self.v_gain is not None:\n            out = out * self.v_gain[i]\n        x = x + self.s_attn[i] * F.linear(out, self.proj)\n        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]\n        if self.mlp_type == \"swiglu\":\n            a, b = F.linear(h, self.fc.flatten(0, 1)).chunk(2, dim=-1)\n            u = F.silu(a) * b\n        elif self.mlp_type == \"relu2\":\n            pre = F.linear(h, self.fc[0])\n            if self.lora_fc_up is not None:\n                pre = pre + F.linear(F.linear(h, self.lora_fc_down[i]), self.lora_fc_up[i])\n            if self.hid_bias is not None:\n                pre = pre + self.hid_bias[i]\n            u = F.relu(pre).square()\n        else:\n            u = F.gelu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0]), approximate=\"tanh\")\n        if self.hid_gain is not None:\n            u = u * self.hid_gain[i]\n        w_out = self.fc[0].t() if self.fc_out is None else self.fc_out\n        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, w_out), v0\n\n\nclass LoopedLM(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.cfg = cfg\n        d = cfg.d_model\n        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)\n        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)\n        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_unique))\n        self.mem_at = tuple(cfg.mem_at)\n        if self.mem_at:\n            self.mem_g = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(len(self.mem_at), d))\n            self.mem_s = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(len(self.mem_at), d))\n        self.smear = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d)) if cfg.smear else None\n        if cfg.dense:\n            n = cfg.n_loops\n            init = torch.zeros(n, n + 1)\n            init[torch.arange(n), torch.arange(1, n + 1)] = 1.0\n            self.dwa = nn.Parameter(init)\n        else:\n            self.dwa = None\n        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))\n        self.out_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))\n        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads\n        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)\n        self.register_buffer(\"inv_freq\", inv_freq, persistent=False)\n        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py may replace this with a compiled version\n\n    def apply_blocks(self, x, cos, sin):\n        x0 = x\n        v0 = None\n        table = self.embed.weight\n        history = [x0]\n        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):\n            for block in self.blocks:\n                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)\n            if self.dwa is not None:\n                history.append(x)\n                x = sum(self.dwa[i, j] * h for j, h in enumerate(history))\n            if i in self.mem_at:\n                j = self.mem_at.index(i)\n                scores = F.linear(_norm(x) * self.mem_g[j], table) * self.cfg.mem_temp\n                if self.cfg.mem_act == \"softmax\":\n                    weights = torch.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).type_as(scores)\n                else:\n                    weights = F.relu(scores).square()\n                x = x + self.mem_s[j] * F.linear(weights, table.t())\n        return _norm(x) * self.out_gain\n\n    def forward(self, input_ids):\n        length = input_ids.shape[1]\n        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)\n        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]\n        x = _norm(self.embed(input_ids)) * self.in_scale\n        if self.smear is not None:\n            x = x + self.smear * F.pad(x[:, :-1], (0, 0, 1, 0))\n        return self.stack(x, angles.cos(), angles.sin())  # the harness applies the tied output head\n\n\ndef build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:\n    \"\"\"config is config.json plus {\"vocab_size\": ...} from the harness.\"\"\"\n\n    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config[\"vocab_size\"], **config.get(\"model\", {})))\n", "numLines": 222, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 222}}

sed -n 60,130p submission/train.py
cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    body = model.inner.body
    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        body.stack = torch.compile(body.apply_blocks)

    matrix_names = ("qkv", "kv", "proj", "fc", "fc_out")
    matrices = [p for n, p in body.named_parameters() if n.startswith("blocks.") and n.split(".")[-1] in matrix_names]
    embed = [body.embed.weight]
    ids = {id(p) for p in matrices + embed}
    lora = [p for n, p in body.named_parameters() if "lora" in n]
    ids |= {id(p) for p in lora}
    others = [p for p in body.parameters() if id(p) not in ids]
    params = list(body.parameters())
    muon = Muon(matrices, lr=cfg["muon_lr"], momentum=cfg.get("muon_momentum", 0.95),
                weight_decay=cfg.get("muon_wd", 0.0))
    adam = torch.optim.AdamW([
        {"params": embed, "lr": cfg["embed_lr"], "weight_decay": cfg.get("embed_wd", 0.0)},
        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
        {"params": lora, "lr": cfg.get("lora_lr", 0.003), "weight_decay": 0.0},
    ], betas=(0.9, 0.95), eps=1e-10, fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")
    for opt in (muon, adam):
        for group in opt.param_groups:
            group["base_lr"] = group["lr"]

    warmup, cooldown, final = cfg["warmup"], cfg["cooldown"], cfg.get("final_lr_frac", 0.0)

    def lr_factor(progress: float) -> float:
        if progress < warmup:
            return max(progress, 1e-3) / warmup
        if progress > 1 - cooldown:
            return final + (1 - final) * max(0.0, (1 - progress) / cooldown)
        return 1.0

    micro, accum = cfg["micro_batch"], cfg["accumulation"]
    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        f = lr_factor(budget.fraction_used)
        for opt in (muon, adam):
            for group in opt.param_groups:
                group["lr"] = group["base_lr"] * f
        total = torch.zeros((), device=ctx.device)
        for _ in range(accum):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / accum).backward()
            total += loss.detach() / accum
        grads = [p.grad for p in params if p.grad is not None]
        if cfg.get("clip", 0) > 0:
            norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, cfg["clip"])
        else:
            norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(torch.stack(torch._foreach_norm(grads)))
        # safety: a non-finite gradient (one bad batch) zeroes this update instead of corrupting the weights
        finite = torch.isfinite(norm)
        for g in grads:
            torch.where(finite, g, torch.zeros_like(g), out=g)
        muon.step()
        adam.step()
        muon.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        adam.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            value = total.item()
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": value, "lr_factor": f})

        if self.canon_a is not None:
            h = h + _causal_conv(h, self.canon_a[i])
""")
s = s.replace("""        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]
""", """        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]
        if self.canon_m is not None:
            h = h + _causal_conv(h, self.canon_m[i])
""")
s = s.replace("""def _norm(x):""", """def _causal_conv(h, w):
    \"\"\"Depthwise causal convolution over positions: sum_j w[j] * h[t - j] (zero before the start).\"\"\"
    out = w[0] * h
    for j in range(1, w.shape[0]):
        out = out + w[j] * F.pad(h[:, :-j], (0, 0, j, 0))
    return out


def _norm(x):""")
open(p, "w").write(s)

#110 Write/workspace/clean/model.py
"""Looped (depth-recurrent) transformer with a wide, free token embedding.

The weight cap (3M) is tight but the compute cap (120M FLOPs/token) is ten
times what a 3M-weight model needs for one pass, so the model applies one
shared transformer block n_loops times (15 by default: ~111M FLOPs/token)
at the widest width the exempt embedding table allows (512).

To let the loops specialise, every application i of the block has its own
cheap per-loop vectors (~5k numbers per loop, ~80k in total):

* g_attn[i], g_mlp[i]   gains of the pre-attention / pre-MLP RMSNorms
* s_attn[i], s_mlp[i]   per-channel scales of the two residual branches
* inj[i]                re-injection of the input embedding x0
* q_gain[i]             per-head, per-dimension query gain after QK-norm
                        (sets each loop's attention temperature/pattern)
* v_gain[i]             gain on the attention output before the projection
                        (per-head gating)
* hid_gain[i]           gain on each MLP hidden unit (which units a loop uses)
* v_mix[i]              value residual: mixes in the first loop's values

and after every loop a DenseFormer-style learned weighted average over the
embedding and all earlier loop outputs (dwa, n_loops x (n_loops+1) scalars)
forms the next loop's input.

Block: RMSNorm -> causal attention (8 heads, RoPE, QK-norm) -> RMSNorm ->
ReLU^2 MLP; no biases; projections into the residual stream start at zero.
The model returns the final normalised hidden states; the harness turns them
into logits with the token embedding table (tied output head).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn


@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 512
    n_heads: int = 8
    n_unique: int = 1  # distinct blocks, applied in sequence once per loop
    n_loops: int = 15
    mlp_width: int = 1814
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    embed_std: float = 0.02
    value_residual: bool = True
    dense: bool = True


def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)


def _norm(x):
    return F.rms_norm(x, (x.shape[-1],))


class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        d, n = cfg.d_model, cfg.n_loops
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
        # shared weights (trained with Muon)
        self.qkv = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(3, d, d))
        self.proj = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, d))
        self.fc = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(1, cfg.mlp_width, d))
        self.fc_out = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(d, cfg.mlp_width))
        # per-loop parameters (trained with Adam)
        self.g_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
        self.g_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
        self.s_attn = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
        self.s_mlp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
        self.inj = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d))
        self.v_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(n, d)) if cfg.value_residual else None
        self.hid_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, cfg.mlp_width))
        self.v_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, d))
        self.q_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(n, self.n_heads, 1, self.head_dim))
        bound = 0.5 / math.sqrt(d) * math.sqrt(3)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.qkv, -bound, bound)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.fc, -bound, bound)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.proj)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.fc_out)

    def forward(self, x, x0, cos, sin, i: int, v0=None):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        x = x + self.inj[i] * x0
        h = _norm(x) * self.g_attn[i]
        q, k, v = F.linear(h, self.qkv.flatten(0, 1)).split(width, dim=2)
        if self.v_mix is not None:
            if v0 is None:
                v0 = v
            else:
                v = v + self.v_mix[i] * (v0 - v)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        q, k = _norm(q) * self.q_gain[i], _norm(k)
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        out = out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width) * self.v_gain[i]
        x = x + self.s_attn[i] * F.linear(out, self.proj)
        h = _norm(x) * self.g_mlp[i]
        u = F.relu(F.linear(h, self.fc[0])).square() * self.hid_gain[i]
        return x + self.s_mlp[i] * F.linear(u, self.fc_out), v0


class LoopedLM(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        d, n = cfg.d_model, cfg.n_loops
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, d)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=cfg.embed_std)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_unique))
        if cfg.dense:
            init = torch.zeros(n, n + 1)
            init[torch.arange(n), torch.arange(1, n + 1)] = 1.0  # starts as a plain residual stream
            self.dwa = nn.Parameter(init)
        else:
            self.dwa = None
        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
        self.out_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))
        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version

    def apply_blocks(self, x, cos, sin):
        x0, v0 = x, None
        history = [x0]
        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):
            for block in self.blocks:
                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)
            if self.dwa is not None:
                history.append(x)
                x = sum(self.dwa[i, j] * h for j, h in enumerate(history))
        return _norm(x) * self.out_gain

    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        x = _norm(self.embed(input_ids)) * self.in_scale
        return self.stack(x, angles.cos(), angles.sin())  # the harness applies the tied output head


def build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""

    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))

nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_unique))\n        if cfg.dense:\n            init = torch.zeros(n, n + 1)\n            init[torch.arange(n), torch.arange(1, n + 1)] = 1.0  # starts as a plain residual stream\n            self.dwa = nn.Parameter(init)\n        else:\n            self.dwa = None\n        self.in_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))\n        self.out_gain = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(d))\n        head_dim = d // cfg.n_heads\n        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)\n        self.register_buffer(\"inv_freq\", inv_freq, persistent=False)\n        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version\n\n    def apply_blocks(self, x, cos, sin):\n        x0, v0 = x, None\n        history = [x0]\n        for i in range(self.cfg.n_loops):\n            for block in self.blocks:\n                x, v0 = block(x, x0, cos, sin, i, v0)\n            if self.dwa is not None:\n                history.append(x)\n                x = sum(self.dwa[i, j] * h for j, h in enumerate(history))\n        return _norm(x) * self.out_gain\n\n    def forward(self, input_ids):\n        length = input_ids.shape[1]\n        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)\n        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]\n        x = _norm(self.embed(input_ids)) * self.in_scale\n        return self.stack(x, angles.cos(), angles.sin())  # the harness applies the tied output head\n\n\ndef build_model(config: dict) -> LoopedLM:\n    \"\"\"config is config.json plus {\"vocab_size\": ...} from the harness.\"\"\"\n\n    return LoopedLM(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config[\"vocab_size\"], **config.get(\"model\", {})))\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

"""Training recipe: next-token loss with Muon (hidden matrices) + Adam (embedding, gains).

Random 512-token windows, bf16 autocast, a short warmup, a constant phase
and a linear cooldown at the end. Matrices of the transformer blocks are
updated with Muon (orthogonalised momentum, Newton-Schulz iteration); the
token embedding and the per-loop gain vectors with Adam. Training stops when
the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F


def _orthogonalize(G, steps: int = 5):
    a, b, c = (3.4445, -4.7750, 2.0315)
    X = G.bfloat16()
    transposed = G.size(-2) > G.size(-1)
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    X = X / (X.norm(dim=(-2, -1), keepdim=True) + 1e-7)
    for _ in range(steps):
        A = X @ X.mT
        B = b * A + c * A @ A
        X = a * X + B @ X
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    return X


class Muon(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    def __init__(self, params, lr=0.02, momentum=0.95, weight_decay=0.0, nesterov=True):
        super().__init__(params, dict(lr=lr, momentum=momentum, weight_decay=weight_decay, nesterov=nesterov))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            lr, mom, wd = group["lr"], group["momentum"], group["weight_decay"]
            for p in group["params"]:
                if p.grad is None:
                    continue
                g = p.grad
                state = self.state[p]
                if "buf" not in state:
                    state["buf"] = torch.zeros_like(g)
                buf = state["buf"]
                buf.lerp_(g, 1 - mom)
                g = g.lerp(buf, mom) if group["nesterov"] else buf
                u = _orthogonalize(g)
                u = u * max(1.0, p.size(-2) / p.size(-1)) ** 0.5
                if wd:
                    p.mul_(1 - lr * wd)
                p.add_(u.to(p.dtype), alpha=-lr)

#112 Write/workspace/clean/train.py
"""Training recipe: next-token loss with Muon (block matrices) + Adam (embedding, per-loop gains).

Random 512-token windows (16k tokens per update), bf16 autocast, a 2% warmup,
a constant phase and a linear cooldown to zero over the last 40% of the
budget. The shared block's weight matrices are updated with Muon
(Nesterov momentum 0.9, orthogonalised by a Newton-Schulz iteration,
decoupled weight decay); the token embedding and all per-loop vectors and
mixing weights with Adam. A non-finite gradient zeroes that update instead of
corrupting the weights. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

MATRIX_NAMES = ("qkv", "proj", "fc", "fc_out")


def _orthogonalize(G, steps: int = 5):
    """Approximately replace G by the nearest semi-orthogonal matrix (batched over leading dims)."""

    a, b, c = (3.4445, -4.7750, 2.0315)
    X = G.bfloat16()
    transposed = G.size(-2) > G.size(-1)
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    X = X / (X.norm(dim=(-2, -1), keepdim=True) + 1e-7)
    for _ in range(steps):
        A = X @ X.mT
        B = b * A + c * A @ A
        X = a * X + B @ X
    if transposed:
        X = X.mT
    return X


class Muon(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    def __init__(self, params, lr=0.02, momentum=0.95, weight_decay=0.0, nesterov=True):
        super().__init__(params, dict(lr=lr, momentum=momentum, weight_decay=weight_decay, nesterov=nesterov))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            lr, mom, wd = group["lr"], group["momentum"], group["weight_decay"]
            for p in group["params"]:
                if p.grad is None:
                    continue
                g = p.grad
                state = self.state[p]
                if "buf" not in state:
                    state["buf"] = torch.zeros_like(g)
                buf = state["buf"]
                buf.lerp_(g, 1 - mom)
                g = g.lerp(buf, mom) if group["nesterov"] else buf
                u = _orthogonalize(g)
                u = u * max(1.0, p.size(-2) / p.size(-1)) ** 0.5
                if wd:
                    p.mul_(1 - lr * wd)
                p.add_(u.to(p.dtype), alpha=-lr)


def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    body = model.inner.body
    if cfg.get("compile", True) and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        body.stack = torch.compile(body.apply_blocks)

    params = list(body.parameters())
    matrices = [p for n, p in body.named_parameters() if n.startswith("blocks.") and n.split(".")[-1] in MATRIX_NAMES]
    embed = [body.embed.weight]
    taken = {id(p) for p in matrices + embed}
    others = [p for p in params if id(p) not in taken]
    muon = Muon(matrices, lr=cfg["muon_lr"], momentum=cfg["muon_momentum"], weight_decay=cfg["muon_wd"])
    adam = torch.optim.AdamW([
        {"params": embed, "lr": cfg["embed_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
        {"params": others, "lr": cfg["scalar_lr"], "weight_decay": 0.0},
    ], betas=(0.9, 0.95), eps=1e-10, fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")
    for opt in (muon, adam):
        for group in opt.param_groups:
            group["base_lr"] = group["lr"]

    warmup, cooldown = cfg["warmup"], cfg["cooldown"]

    def lr_factor(progress: float) -> float:
        if progress < warmup:
            return max(progress, 1e-3) / warmup
        if progress > 1 - cooldown:
            return max(0.0, (1 - progress) / cooldown)
        return 1.0

    micro, accum = cfg["micro_batch"], cfg["accumulation"]
    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        f = lr_factor(budget.fraction_used)
        for opt in (muon, adam):
            for group in opt.param_groups:
                group["lr"] = group["base_lr"] * f
        total = torch.zeros((), device=ctx.device)
        for _ in range(accum):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(micro)
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / accum).backward()
            total += loss.detach() / accum
        grads = [p.grad for p in params if p.grad is not None]
        norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(torch.stack(torch._foreach_norm(grads)))
        finite = torch.isfinite(norm)  # stays on the GPU: no synchronisation
        for g in grads:
            g.copy_(torch.where(finite, g, 0.0))
        muon.step()
        adam.step()
        muon.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        adam.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": total.item(), "lr_factor": f})

import sys, json, torch, importlib.util
from lmenv.harness import OutputHead, unique_weights, flops_per_token
def load(d, name):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, d + "/model.py")
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[name] = m; spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    cfg = json.load(open(d + "/config.json")); cfg["vocab_size"] = 8192
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    return OutputHead(m.build_model(cfg), 8192).cuda().eval()
old = load("variants/F3_full", "old_model")
new = load("clean", "new_model")
so, sn = old.state_dict(), new.state_dict()
print("keys equal:", set(so) == set(sn), set(so) ^ set(sn))
print("init equal:", all(torch.equal(so[k], sn[k]) for k in so))
w = torch.load("runs/F3_full/model.pt", map_location="cuda")
old.load_state_dict(w); new.load_state_dict(w)
ids = torch.randint(0, 8192, (2, 512)).cuda()
with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
    a, b = old(ids).float(), new(ids).float()

#116 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/looped-lm-findings.md
---
name: looped-lm-findings
description: What worked/failed for the 3M-weight, 120M-FLOP LM task in /workspace (lmenv harness), with full-run numbers
metadata:
  type: project
---

Task (2026-11-08): minimise val loss of a <=3M-weight LM (embedding 8192x512 free), <=120M FLOPs/token, 250M tokens.
Starter 3.644. Best full run so far 3.365 (seed 0) with: one shared d512 block looped 15x (ReLU^2 MLP 1814),
per-loop gain vectors (norm gains, residual scales, x0 inject, per-dim q gain, head gating, MLP hidden-unit gains),
DenseFormer mixing over loop outputs, value residual, Muon (lr .015, mom .9, wd .03) + Adam (embed .01, per-loop .03),
16k-token batches, 2% warmup, 40% linear cooldown.

**Why:** params bind, compute doesn't: 8 loops only ~0.01 worse than 15 at full scale.
**How to apply:** 50M-token proxies over-reward small batches/fast-learning tweaks (16k vs 32k batch: -0.02 proxy, 0 full);
proxy noise ~0.005. Failed: embedding-as-memory layer, tied MLP, GQA, d384, SwiGLU, smear, LoRA per loop, canon convs.
Bug to avoid: routing [loops, d] gain tensors to Muon via `p.dim() >= 2` (fixing it gave -0.013).

#137
3h 22m

The submission is finished and passes all checks. On the full training budget it reaches a validation loss of 3.357 (1.244 bits per byte), averaged over two seeds (3.3578 and 3.3562). The starting point scores 3.644, so that is about 0.29 nats lower. No jobs are still running.

The model (submission/)

  • One shared block, run 16 times. The block is 512 wide, the most the free embedding table allows. It has 8-head causal attention and a ReLU² MLP of width 1810. Totals: 2,997,840 counted weights and 118.0M FLOPs per token, against caps of 3M and 120M.
  • Cheap per-loop parameters (about 80k numbers in all) let each of the 16 passes behave differently. Each pass gets its own norm gains, residual scales, re-injection of the input embedding, query gains, per-head output gates, MLP hidden-unit gains and a value-residual mix.
  • Learned mixing of earlier passes. The input to each pass is a learned weighted average of the embedding and every earlier pass's output (DenseFormer-style).
  • Training:
    • Muon (learning rate 0.015, momentum 0.9, weight decay 0.03) for the block's weight matrices, and Adam for the embedding (0.01) and the per-loop parameters (0.03).
    • 16k-token batches, a 2% warmup, then a linear decay to zero over the last half of training.
    • An update is skipped if its gradients come out non-finite, so one bad batch on an unseen text can't wreck a run.

What the gains came from (full-budget runs)

StepValidation loss
Starting point3.644
Looped 512-wide model, first recipe3.456
Learning rates tuned, per-loop gains, optimizer bug fixed3.381
+ mixing of earlier passes, momentum 0.9, embedding LR 0.013.368
+ value residual, Muon weight decay (3 seeds: 3.3652, 3.3654, 3.3691)3.367 mean
+ 16 passes, 50% decay phase (final; 2 seeds)3.357 mean

Things I learned along the way:

  • Optimizer bug. My first version accidentally trained the per-loop gain vectors with Muon instead of Adam. Fixing it was worth 0.013 in the short test runs.
  • Weights are the limit, not compute. At full budget, 8 passes were only about 0.01 worse than 15.
  • Short test runs overstate gains. I screened ideas on 50M tokens, a fifth of the budget. Smaller batches looked 0.02 better there and gave nothing at full budget, and other gains shrank by roughly 3–4×. So I confirmed every change I kept on the full budget.
  • Tried and dropped: using the embedding table as an extra memory layer, a transposed-weight MLP, shared key/value heads, a narrower 384-wide model, SwiGLU, mixing in the previous token, per-loop low-rank adapters, short causal convolutions, and Muon learning rate 0.02 at full budget.

Caveats

  • The final settings rest on two seeds. The two changes behind them (16 passes and the longer decay) each looked good on a single seed first, and together they improved both seeds by about 0.008.
  • All tuning was on your dev text, so results on your two held-out texts may differ somewhat. Expect about ±0.004 between seeds.
  • A full training run takes about 15–20 minutes on the H200.

Versions are saved in snapshots/001 to 005; 005 matches the current submission/.

I used the extra time for one more change: the learning rate now decays to zero over the last 65% of training instead of 50%. That brought the full-budget validation loss to 3.353 (mean of seeds 0 and 1: 3.3544 and 3.3562→3.3523), down from 3.357. The starting point scores 3.644, so the submission is now about 0.29 nats lower.

The decay change improved both seeds by about 0.004 each. Only config.json and one line of the training docstring changed; the model code is the same. Checks still pass at 2,997,840 weights and 118.0M FLOPs per token.

Progress of the decay phase, same two seeds:

Decay over lastSeed 0Seed 1Mean
40% (15 loops)3.36523.36543.3653
50% (16 loops)3.35783.35623.3570
65% (16 loops, final)3.35443.35233.3533

The trend suggests an even longer decay might help a little more. I didn't try it because a pair of full runs takes about 18 minutes and only 19 were left.

The final version is saved as snapshots/006 and matches submission/. Nothing is still running.
